===== app/Console/Commands/FifoBackfill.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLot;
use App\Models\Expense;
use App\Services\InventoryFifoService;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

/**
 * One-time opening lots for stock that already exists (pre-FIFO).
 * Opening cost is taken from the item's most recent ingredient expense
 * (amount / quantity); if none is found it defaults to 0 and can be fixed
 * later. Safe to re-run: items that already have a lot are skipped.
 */
class FifoBackfill extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'trove:fifo-backfill {--date= : opening date (default today)}';
    protected $description = 'Create opening FIFO lots for current inventory on-hand';

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $date = $this->option('date') ?: now()->toDateString();
        $made = 0; $skipped = 0;

        foreach (Inventory::all() as $inv) {
            if (InventoryLot::where('inventory_id', $inv->id)->exists()) { $skipped++; continue; }
            $onHand = (float) $inv->quantity_on_hand;
            if ($onHand <= 0) { $skipped++; continue; }

            $lastExpense = Expense::where('inventory_id', $inv->id)
                ->whereNull('voided_at')->where('quantity', '>', 0)
                ->orderByDesc('expense_date')->orderByDesc('id')->first();
            $unitCost = $lastExpense ? round((float) $lastExpense->amount / (float) $lastExpense->quantity, 2) : 0.0;

            InventoryFifoService::receive($inv->id, $onHand, $unitCost, $date, null);
            $made++;
            $this->line("  opening lot: {$inv->item_name}  x{$onHand} @ {$unitCost}");
        }

        $this->info("Done. Opening lots created: {$made}, skipped: {$skipped}.");
        return self::SUCCESS;
    }
}

===== app/Http/AppServiceProvider.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Providers;

use Illuminate\Support\ServiceProvider;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\URL;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Gate;

class AppServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider
{
    /**
     * Register any application services.
     */
    public function register(): void
    {
        //
    }

    /**
     * Bootstrap any application services.
     */
    public function boot(): void
    {
        if (env('APP_ENV') === 'production') {
            URL::forceScheme('https');
        }

        // Admin gate — Owner and Manager can manage products, inventory, etc.
        // This makes @can('admin') work in Blade views.
        Gate::define('admin', function ($user) {
            return in_array($user->role, ['Owner', 'Manager']);
        });
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/AuthenticatedSessionController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class AuthenticatedSessionController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the login view.
     */
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.login');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming authentication request.
     */
    public function store(LoginRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->authenticate();

        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }

    /**
     * Destroy an authenticated session.
     */
    public function destroy(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        Auth::guard('web')->logout();

        $request->session()->invalidate();

        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect('/');
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/ConfirmablePasswordController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class ConfirmablePasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Show the confirm password view.
     */
    public function show(): View
    {
        return view('auth.confirm-password');
    }

    /**
     * Confirm the user's password.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if (! Auth::guard('web')->validate([
            'email' => $request->user()->email,
            'password' => $request->password,
        ])) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'password' => __('auth.password'),
            ]);
        }

        $request->session()->put('auth.password_confirmed_at', time());

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/EmailVerificationNotificationController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class EmailVerificationNotificationController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Send a new email verification notification.
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false));
        }

        $request->user()->sendEmailVerificationNotification();

        return back()->with('status', 'verification-link-sent');
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/EmailVerificationPromptController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class EmailVerificationPromptController extends Controller

                    ? redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false))
                    : view('auth.verify-email');
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/NewPasswordController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\PasswordReset;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class NewPasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset view.
     */
    public function create(Request $request): View
    {
        return view('auth.reset-password', ['request' => $request]);
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming new password request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'token' => ['required'],
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
            'password' => ['required', 'confirmed', Rules\Password::defaults()],
        ]);

        // Here we will attempt to reset the user's password. If it is successful we
        // will update the password on an actual user model and persist it to the
        // database. Otherwise we will parse the error and return the response.
        $status = Password::reset(
            $request->only('email', 'password', 'password_confirmation', 'token'),
            function (User $user) use ($request) {
                $user->forceFill([
                    'password' => Hash::make($request->password),
                    'remember_token' => Str::random(60),
                ])->save();

                event(new PasswordReset($user));
            }
        );

        // If the password was successfully reset, we will redirect the user back to
        // the application's home authenticated view. If there is an error we can
        // redirect them back to where they came from with their error message.
        return $status == Password::PASSWORD_RESET
                    ? redirect()->route('login')->with('status', __($status))
                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rules\Password;

class PasswordController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Update the user's password.
     */
    public function update(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $validated = $request->validateWithBag('updatePassword', [
            'current_password' => ['required', 'current_password'],
            'password' => ['required', Password::defaults(), 'confirmed'],
        ]);

        $request->user()->update([
            'password' => Hash::make($validated['password']),
        ]);

        return back()->with('status', 'password-updated');
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordResetLinkController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class PasswordResetLinkController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Display the password reset link request view.
     */
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.forgot-password');
    }

    /**
     * Handle an incoming password reset link request.
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
        ]);

        // We will send the password reset link to this user. Once we have attempted
        // to send the link, we will examine the response then see the message we
        // need to show to the user. Finally, we'll send out a proper response.
        $status = Password::sendResetLink(
            $request->only('email')
        );

        return $status == Password::RESET_LINK_SENT
                    ? back()->with('status', __($status))
                    : back()->withInput($request->only('email'))
                        ->withErrors(['email' => __($status)]);
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/RegisteredUserController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Registered;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\View\View;

class RegisteredUserController extends Controller
{
    public function create(): View
    {
        return view('auth.register');
    }

   public function store(Request $request): RedirectResponse
{
    $validated = $request->validate([
        'name'     => 'required|string|max:255',
        'email'    => 'required|string|email|max:255|unique:users',
        'password' => 'required|confirmed|min:8',
    ]);

    // Split the single "Full Name" field into first/last, since that's
    // how the users table is actually structured
    $nameParts = explode(' ', trim($validated['name']), 2);
    $firstName = $nameParts[0];
    $lastName  = $nameParts[1] ?? '';

    $user = User::create([
        'first_name' => $firstName,
        'last_name'  => $lastName,
        'email'      => $validated['email'],
        'password'   => Hash::make($validated['password']),
        'role'       => 'Staff',
    ]);

    event(new Registered($user));
    Auth::login($user);

    return redirect()->route('dashboard');
}
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/Auth/VerifyEmailController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Auth\Events\Verified;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\EmailVerificationRequest;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;

class VerifyEmailController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Mark the authenticated user's email address as verified.
     */
    public function __invoke(EmailVerificationRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if ($request->user()->hasVerifiedEmail()) {
            return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
        }

        if ($request->user()->markEmailAsVerified()) {
            event(new Verified($request->user()));
        }

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard', absolute: false).'?verified=1');
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/BranchOrderController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\BranchOrder;
use App\Models\BranchOrderItem;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BranchOrderController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $open = BranchOrder::decorate(
            BranchOrder::with(['items.product', 'destinationSite'])
                ->whereIn('status', ['requested', 'produced', 'partial'])
                ->orderBy('needed_by')->get()
        );

        $recent = BranchOrder::decorate(
            BranchOrder::with(['items.product', 'destinationSite'])
                ->whereIn('status', ['complete', 'cancelled'])
                ->orderBy('updated_at', 'desc')->limit(15)->get()
        );

        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();

        return redirect()->route('branch-transfers.index');
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $v = $request->validate([
            'destination_site_id' => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'needed_by'           => 'required|date',
            'notes'               => 'nullable|string|max:500',
            'items'               => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.product_id'  => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.qty'         => 'required|integer|min:1',
        ]);

        DB::transaction(function () use ($v) {
            $order = BranchOrder::create([
                'destination_site_id' => $v['destination_site_id'] ?? null,
                'needed_by'           => $v['needed_by'],
                'notes'               => $v['notes'] ?? null,
                'status'              => 'requested',
                'created_by'          => auth()->id(),
            ]);
            foreach ($v['items'] as $it) {
                $order->items()->create(['product_id' => $it['product_id'], 'qty' => $it['qty']]);
            }
            $this->audit('branch_order.requested', $order, ['needed_by' => $v['needed_by'], 'items' => count($v['items'])]);
        });

        return redirect()->route('branch-transfers.index')->with('success', 'Order recorded.');
    }

    public function produce(BranchOrder $order)
    {
        if ($order->status === 'requested') {
            $order->update(['status' => 'produced']);
            $this->audit('branch_order.produced', $order, []);
        }
        return back()->with('success', 'Order marked as produced.');
    }

    public function cancel(BranchOrder $order)
    {
        if (in_array($order->status, ['requested', 'produced'], true)) {
            $order->update(['status' => 'cancelled']);
            $this->audit('branch_order.cancelled', $order, []);
            return back()->with('success', 'Order cancelled.');
        }
        return back()->with('error', 'Only orders with nothing sent yet can be cancelled. Use Close short instead.');
    }

    /** Stop waiting for the remaining balance. The balance is not sold and earns no revenue. */
    public function closeShort(Request $request, BranchOrder $order)
    {
        $v = $request->validate(['reason' => 'required|string|min:3|max:500']);

        if (!in_array($order->status, ['requested', 'produced', 'partial'], true)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This order is already closed.');
        }

        $fresh = BranchOrder::with('items')->find($order->id);
        BranchOrder::decorate(collect([$fresh]));
        $short = $fresh->balance_qty;

        DB::transaction(function () use ($fresh, $v) {
            foreach ($fresh->items as $it) {
                if ($it->balance > 0) {
                    BranchOrderItem::whereKey($it->id)->update(['qty_closed' => (int) $it->qty_closed + $it->balance]);
                }
            }
            BranchOrder::whereKey($fresh->id)->update(['status' => 'complete', 'short_reason' => $v['reason']]);
        });

        $this->audit('branch_order.closed_short', $order, ['short_qty' => $short, 'reason' => $v['reason']]);

        return back()->with('success', "Order closed. {$short} pcs not delivered.");
    }

    private function audit(string $action, $model, array $data): void
    {
        try {
            if (class_exists(\App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::class)) {
                \App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::record($action, $model, $data);
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            \Log::warning('Audit log failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/BranchOrderController.php.bak-branch-merge =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\BranchOrder;
use App\Models\BranchOrderItem;

===== app/Http/Controllers/BranchOrderController.php.bak-branch-orders2 =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\BranchOrder;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BranchOrderController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $open = BranchOrder::with(['items.product', 'destinationSite'])
            ->whereIn('status', ['requested', 'produced'])
            ->orderBy('needed_by')->get();

        $recent = BranchOrder::with(['items.product', 'destinationSite'])
            ->whereIn('status', ['sent', 'cancelled'])
            ->orderBy('updated_at', 'desc')->limit(15)->get();

        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();

        return view('branch-orders.index', compact('open', 'recent', 'products', 'sites'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $v = $request->validate([
            'destination_site_id' => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'needed_by'           => 'required|date',
            'notes'               => 'nullable|string|max:500',
            'items'               => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.product_id'  => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.qty'         => 'required|integer|min:1',
        ]);

        DB::transaction(function () use ($v) {
            $order = BranchOrder::create([
                'destination_site_id' => $v['destination_site_id'] ?? null,
                'needed_by'           => $v['needed_by'],
                'notes'               => $v['notes'] ?? null,
                'status'              => 'requested',
                'created_by'          => auth()->id(),
            ]);
            foreach ($v['items'] as $it) {
                $order->items()->create(['product_id' => $it['product_id'], 'qty' => $it['qty']]);
            }
            $this->audit('branch_order.requested', $order, ['needed_by' => $v['needed_by'], 'items' => count($v['items'])]);
        });

        return redirect()->route('branch-orders.index')->with('success', 'Order recorded.');
    }

    public function produce(BranchOrder $order)
    {
        if ($order->status === 'requested') {
            $order->update(['status' => 'produced']);
            $this->audit('branch_order.produced', $order, []);
        }
        return back()->with('success', 'Order marked as produced.');
    }

    public function cancel(BranchOrder $order)
    {
        if (in_array($order->status, ['requested', 'produced'], true)) {
            $order->update(['status' => 'cancelled']);
            $this->audit('branch_order.cancelled', $order, []);
        }
        return back()->with('success', 'Order cancelled.');
    }

    private function audit(string $action, $model, array $data): void
    {
        try {
            if (class_exists(\App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::class)) {
                \App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::record($action, $model, $data);
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            \Log::warning('Audit log failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/BranchTransferController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Batch;
use App\Models\BatchItem;
use App\Models\BranchOrder;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BranchTransferController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $batches = Batch::with(['items', 'sourceSite', 'destinationSite'])
            ->orderBy('batch_date', 'desc')
            ->get();

        $orders = BranchOrder::decorate(
            BranchOrder::with(['items.product', 'destinationSite'])
                ->orderBy('needed_by', 'desc')->limit(20)->get()
        );

        return $this->board($batches);
    }

    private function board($batches)
    {
        $pending = BranchOrder::with(['items.product', 'destinationSite'])
            ->whereIn('status', ['requested', 'produced', 'partial'])
            ->orderBy('needed_by')->get();
        $done = BranchOrder::with(['items.product', 'destinationSite'])
            ->whereIn('status', ['complete', 'cancelled'])
            ->orderBy('needed_by', 'desc')->limit(10)->get();
        $orders = BranchOrder::decorate($pending->concat($done));

        // Note on the latest live batch of each order that is still short (or was closed short)
        $linked = $batches->filter(fn ($b) => $b->branch_order_id && empty($b->voided_at))
            ->sortByDesc(fn ($b) => $b->batch_date->format('Y-m-d') . str_pad((string) $b->id, 10, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT));
        $byOrder = BranchOrder::decorate(
            BranchOrder::with('items')->whereIn('id', $linked->pluck('branch_order_id')->unique()->all())->get()
        )->keyBy('id');

        $notes = [];
        $seen = [];
        foreach ($linked as $b) {
            $oid = $b->branch_order_id;
            if (isset($seen[$oid])) {
                continue;
            }
            $seen[$oid] = true;
            $o = $byOrder[$oid] ?? null;
            if (!$o) {
                continue;
            }
            if ($o->balance_qty > 0) {
                $notes[$b->id] = "Short {$o->balance_qty} (order #{$oid}): to be delivered in the next batch";
            } elseif ($o->short_reason) {
                $notes[$b->id] = 'Closed short ' . $o->items->sum('qty_closed') . " (order #{$oid}): " . $o->short_reason;
            }
        }

        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();

        return view('branch-transfers.index', compact('batches', 'orders', 'notes', 'products', 'sites'));
    }

    public function create(Request $request)
    {
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();

        $orders = BranchOrder::decorate(
            BranchOrder::with('items.product')
                ->whereIn('status', ['requested', 'produced', 'partial'])
                ->orderBy('needed_by')->get()
        )->filter(fn ($o) => $o->balance_qty > 0)->values();

        $selected = $request->query('order');

        return view('branch-transfers.create', compact('sites', 'products', 'orders', 'selected'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'source_site_id'      => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'destination_site_id' => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'branch_order_id'     => 'nullable|exists:branch_orders,id',
            'batch_date'          => 'required|date',
            'items'               => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.product_id'  => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.qty_sent'    => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.unit_price'  => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        try {
            DB::beginTransaction();

            foreach ($validated['items'] as $item) {
                $product = Product::findOrFail($item['product_id']);
                if ($item['qty_sent'] > $product->stock_quantity) {
                    throw new \Exception(
                        "Not enough {$product->product_name}: only {$product->stock_quantity} on hand, but you tried to send {$item['qty_sent']}."
                    );
                }
            }

            $batch = Batch::create([
                'source_site_id'      => $validated['source_site_id'] ?? null,
                'destination_site_id' => $validated['destination_site_id'] ?? null,
                'batch_date'          => $validated['batch_date'],
                'status'              => 'sent',
                'created_by'          => auth()->id(),
            ]);

            foreach ($validated['items'] as $item) {
                BatchItem::create([
                    'batch_id'     => $batch->id,
                    'product_id'   => $item['product_id'],
                    'qty_sent'     => $item['qty_sent'],
                    'qty_returned' => 0,
                    'unit_price'   => $item['unit_price'],
                ]);

                Product::where('id', $item['product_id'])->decrement('stock_quantity', $item['qty_sent']);
            }

            if (!empty($validated['branch_order_id'])) {
                DB::table('batches')->where('id', $batch->id)->update(['branch_order_id' => $validated['branch_order_id']]);
                $order = BranchOrder::find($validated['branch_order_id']);
                if ($order) {
                    $order->refreshStatus();
                }
            }

            $this->audit('branch_transfer.sent', $batch, [
                'batch_date' => $validated['batch_date'],
                'items'      => count($validated['items']),
                'order_id'   => $validated['branch_order_id'] ?? null,
            ]);

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('branch-transfers.show', $batch)
                ->with('success', 'Batch sent. This is a final sale and was counted as revenue.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Batch send failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function show(Batch $batch)
    {
        $batch->load(['items.product', 'sourceSite', 'destinationSite', 'creator']);
        $voidInfo = DB::table('batches')->where('id', $batch->id)->first(['voided_at', 'void_reason', 'branch_order_id']);
        return view('branch-transfers.show', compact('batch', 'voidInfo'));
    }

    /** Undo a mistaken send: restore stock, exclude from revenue. Reason required. */
    public function void(Request $request, Batch $batch)
    {
        $request->validate(['reason' => 'required|string|min:3|max:500']);

        $row = DB::table('batches')->where('id', $batch->id)->first();
        if ($row->voided_at) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This batch is already voided.');
        }
        if (\App\Models\PeriodClosure::isClosed($batch->batch_date)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This batch belongs to a closed month and cannot be voided.');
        }

        try {
            DB::beginTransaction();

            foreach ($batch->items as $item) {
                if ($item->product_id) {
                    Product::where('id', $item->product_id)->increment('stock_quantity', $item->qty_sent);
                }
            }

            DB::table('batches')->where('id', $batch->id)->update([
                'voided_at'   => now(),
                'void_reason' => $request->reason,
                'voided_by'   => auth()->id(),
            ]);

            if ($row->branch_order_id) {
                $order = BranchOrder::find($row->branch_order_id);
                if ($order) {
                    $order->refreshStatus();
                }
            }

            $this->audit('branch_transfer.voided', $batch, ['reason' => $request->reason]);

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('branch-transfers.show', $batch)
                ->with('success', 'Batch voided. Stock restored and revenue removed.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Batch void failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->with('error', 'Failed to void: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    private function audit(string $action, $model, array $data): void
    {
        try {
            if (class_exists(\App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::class)) {
                \App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::record($action, $model, $data);
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            \Log::warning('Audit log failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/BranchTransferController.php.bak-branch-final =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Batch;
use App\Models\BatchItem;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BranchTransferController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $batches = Batch::with(['items', 'sourceSite', 'destinationSite'])
            ->orderBy('batch_date', 'desc')
            ->get();

        return view('branch-transfers.index', compact('batches'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();

        return view('branch-transfers.create', compact('sites', 'products'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'source_site_id'      => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'destination_site_id' => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'batch_date'          => 'required|date',
            'items'               => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.product_id'  => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.qty_sent'    => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.unit_price'  => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        try {
            DB::beginTransaction();

            // 1) Check stock FIRST so we never dispatch more than is on hand
            foreach ($validated['items'] as $item) {
                $product = Product::findOrFail($item['product_id']);
                if ($item['qty_sent'] > $product->stock_quantity) {
                    throw new \Exception(
                        "Not enough {$product->product_name}: only {$product->stock_quantity} on hand, but you tried to dispatch {$item['qty_sent']}."
                    );
                }
            }

            // 2) Create the batch and its items, deducting finished stock
            $batch = Batch::create([
                'source_site_id'      => $validated['source_site_id'] ?? null,
                'destination_site_id' => $validated['destination_site_id'] ?? null,
                'batch_date'          => $validated['batch_date'],
                'status'              => 'sent',
                'created_by'          => auth()->id(),
            ]);

            foreach ($validated['items'] as $item) {
                BatchItem::create([
                    'batch_id'     => $batch->id,
                    'product_id'   => $item['product_id'],
                    'qty_sent'     => $item['qty_sent'],
                    'qty_returned' => 0,
                    'unit_price'   => $item['unit_price'],
                ]);

                // finished goods leave Matina
                Product::where('id', $item['product_id'])->decrement('stock_quantity', $item['qty_sent']);
            }

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('branch-transfers.show', $batch)
                ->with('success', 'Batch dispatched and stock updated. Log returns here once Jacinto reports unsold items.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Batch dispatch failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function show(Batch $batch)
    {
        $batch->load(['items.product', 'sourceSite', 'destinationSite', 'creator']);
        return view('branch-transfers.show', compact('batch'));
    }

    public function logReturns(Request $request, Batch $batch)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'returns'   => 'required|array',
            'returns.*' => 'nullable|integer|min:0',
        ]);

        if (\App\Models\PeriodClosure::isClosed($batch->batch_date)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This batch belongs to a closed month; its returns can no longer be changed.');
        }

        try {
            DB::beginTransaction();

            foreach ($batch->items as $item) {
                $newReturned = (int) ($validated['returns'][$item->id] ?? 0);
                $newReturned = min($newReturned, $item->qty_sent); // can't return more than sent

                // how much MORE is coming back vs. what was already logged
                $delta = $newReturned - $item->qty_returned;

                $item->update(['qty_returned' => $newReturned]);

                // returned items come back to Matina's finished stock
                if ($delta !== 0 && $item->product_id) {
                    Product::where('id', $item->product_id)->increment('stock_quantity', $delta);
                }
            }

            $batch->update(['status' => 'reconciled']);

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('branch-transfers.show', $batch)
                ->with('success', 'Returns logged. Net Sold recalculated and returned stock restored to Matina.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Logging returns failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->with('error', 'Failed to log returns: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/BranchTransferController.php.bak-branch-orders2 =====
<?php

use App\Models\Batch;
use App\Models\BatchItem;
use App\Models\BranchOrder;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class BranchTransferController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $batches = Batch::with(['items', 'sourceSite', 'destinationSite'])
            ->orderBy('batch_date', 'desc')
            ->get();

        return view('branch-transfers.index', compact('batches'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $orders = BranchOrder::with('items.product')
            ->whereIn('status', ['requested', 'produced'])
            ->orderBy('needed_by')->get();

        return view('branch-transfers.create', compact('sites', 'products', 'orders'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'source_site_id'      => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'destination_site_id' => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'branch_order_id'     => 'nullable|exists:branch_orders,id',
            'batch_date'          => 'required|date',

        try {

                    throw new \Exception(
                        "Not enough {$product->product_name}: only {$product->stock_quantity} on hand, but you tried to send {$item['qty_sent']}."
                    );
                }
            }

            $batch = Batch::create([
                'source_site_id'      => $validated['source_site_id'] ?? null,
                'destination_site_id' => $validated['destination_site_id'] ?? null,

            if (!empty($validated['branch_order_id'])) {
                DB::table('batches')->where('id', $batch->id)->update(['branch_order_id' => $validated['branch_order_id']]);
                BranchOrder::where('id', $validated['branch_order_id'])->update(['status' => 'sent']);
            }

            $this->audit('branch_transfer.sent', $batch, [
                'batch_date' => $validated['batch_date'],
                'items'      => count($validated['items']),

            return back()->withInput()->with('error', $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function show(Batch $batch)
    {
        $batch->load(['items.product', 'sourceSite', 'destinationSite', 'creator']);

    /** Undo a mistaken send: restore stock, exclude from revenue. Reason required. */
    public function void(Request $request, Batch $batch)
    {

        if ($row->voided_at) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This batch is already voided.');

            foreach ($batch->items as $item) {

                    Product::where('id', $item->product_id)->increment('stock_quantity', $item->qty_sent);
                }
            }

            DB::table('batches')->where('id', $batch->id)->update([
                'voided_at'   => now(),
                'void_reason' => $request->reason,
                'voided_by'   => auth()->id(),
            ]);

            if ($row->branch_order_id) {
                BranchOrder::where('id', $row->branch_order_id)->update(['status' => 'produced']);
            }

            $this->audit('branch_transfer.voided', $batch, ['reason' => $request->reason]);

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('branch-transfers.show', $batch)
                ->with('success', 'Batch voided. Stock restored and revenue removed.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Batch void failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->with('error', 'Failed to void: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    private function audit(string $action, $model, array $data): void
    {
        try {
            if (class_exists(\App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::class)) {
                \App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::record($action, $model, $data);
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            \Log::warning('Audit log failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/CommissionController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Commission;
use App\Models\OrderItem;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use App\Models\FinancialAuditLog;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class CommissionController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $type = $request->input('type', 'all'); // all | individual | institutional

        $query = Commission::with('items.product')->orderBy('created_at', 'desc');
        if (in_array($type, ['individual', 'institutional'])) {
            $query->where('client_type', $type);
        }
        $commissions = $query->get();

        $counts = [
            'all'           => Commission::count(),
            'individual'    => Commission::where('client_type', 'individual')->count(),
            'institutional' => Commission::where('client_type', 'institutional')->count(),
        ];

        return view('commissions.index', compact('commissions', 'type', 'counts'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $sites    = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        return view('commissions.create', compact('products', 'sites'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        // drop blank add-on rows before validating (lets fully-custom orders through)
        $request->merge([
            'items' => array_values(array_filter((array) $request->input('items', []), function ($it) {
                return is_array($it) && !empty($it['product_id']);
            })),
        ]);

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'customer_name'      => 'required|string|max:255',
            'client_type'        => 'nullable|string|max:50',
            'order_type'         => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'needed_by_date'     => 'nullable|date',
            'deposit_amount'     => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'custom_amount'      => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'design_description' => 'nullable|string',
            'site_id'            => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'reference_photo'    => 'nullable|image|max:4096',
            'items'              => 'nullable|array',
            'items.*.product_id' => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.quantity'   => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.price'      => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        $items        = $validated['items'] ?? [];
        $customAmount = $validated['custom_amount'] ?? 0;

        if (count($items) === 0 && $customAmount <= 0) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Add at least one add-on or enter a custom cake price.');
        }

        try {
            $commission = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $validated, $items, $customAmount) {
                $total = (float) $customAmount;
                foreach ($items as $it) {
                    $total += $it['quantity'] * $it['price'];
                }

                $deposit = $validated['deposit_amount'] ?? 0;
                $status  = $deposit > 0 ? 'Confirmed' : 'Inquiry';
                $defaultSiteId = $validated['site_id'] ?? (auth()->user()->site_id ?? Site::query()->value('id'));

                $photoPath = null;
                if ($request->hasFile('reference_photo')) {
                    $photoPath = $request->file('reference_photo')->store('commission-refs', 'local');
                }

                $commission = Commission::create([
                    'user_id'              => auth()->id(),
                    'site_id'              => $defaultSiteId,
                    'customer_name'        => $validated['customer_name'],
                    'client_type'          => $validated['client_type'] ?? 'individual',
                    'order_type'           => $validated['order_type'] ?? 'Customized',
                    'design_description'   => $validated['design_description'] ?? null,
                    'reference_photo_path' => $photoPath,
                    'needed_by_date'       => $validated['needed_by_date'] ?? null,
                    'total_amount'         => $total,
                    'deposit_amount'       => $deposit,
                    'status'               => $status,
                ]);

                // Add-ons are finished catalog products: record them, and (once the
                // order is Confirmed) deduct finished stock, same as a POS sale.
                foreach ($items as $it) {
                    $product = Product::where('id', $it['product_id'])->lockForUpdate()->first();

                    if ($status === 'Confirmed' && (float) $product->stock_quantity < (int) $it['quantity']) {
                        throw new \RuntimeException(
                            "Not enough stock for {$product->product_name} (have {$product->stock_quantity}, need {$it['quantity']})."
                        );
                    }

                    OrderItem::create([
                        'order_id'   => $commission->id,
                        'product_id' => $it['product_id'],
                        'quantity'   => $it['quantity'],
                        'price'      => $it['price'],
                    ]);

                    if ($status === 'Confirmed') {
                        $product->decrement('stock_quantity', (int) $it['quantity']);
                    }
                }

                FinancialAuditLog::record('commission.created', $commission, [
                    'customer' => $commission->customer_name,
                    'total'    => (string) $commission->total_amount,
                    'addons'   => count($items),
                    'status'   => $status,
                ]);

                return $commission;
            });
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Failed to save order: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }

        return redirect()->route('commissions.show', $commission)
            ->with('success', 'Order created successfully.');
    }

    public function show(Commission $commission)
    {
        $commission->load(['items.product', 'site']);
        return view('commissions.show', compact('commission'));
    }

    public function receipt(Commission $commission)
    {
        $commission->load(['items.product', 'site']);
        return view('commissions.receipt', [
            'c'           => $commission,
            'shopName'    => \App\Models\Setting::get('shop_name', 'Trove - Food & Cake Shop'),
            'shopAddress' => \App\Models\Setting::get('shop_address', ''),
            'shopContact' => \App\Models\Setting::get('shop_contact', ''),
            'terms'       => \App\Models\Setting::get('order_terms', \App\Http\Controllers\ShopSettingsController::DEFAULT_TERMS),
        ]);
    }
    public function photo(Commission $commission)
    {
        abort_unless(
            $commission->reference_photo_path && Storage::disk('local')->exists($commission->reference_photo_path),
            404
        );
        return Storage::disk('local')->response($commission->reference_photo_path);
    }

    public function updateStatus(Request $request, Commission $commission)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'status' => 'required|in:' . implode(',', Commission::STAGES),
        ]);
        if ($commission->completed_at && \App\Models\PeriodClosure::isClosed($commission->completed_at)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This order was completed in a closed month and can no longer be changed.');
        }
        $commission->update([
            'status'       => $validated['status'],
            'completed_at' => $validated['status'] === 'Completed' ? ($commission->completed_at ?? now()) : null,
        ]);
        if ($validated['status'] === 'Completed') {
            FinancialAuditLog::record('order.completed', $commission, ['total' => (string) $commission->total_amount]);
        }

        return back()->with('success', 'Status updated to ' . $validated['status'] . '.');
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/CommissionController.php.bakALL =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Commission;
use App\Models\OrderItem;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class CommissionController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $type = $request->input('type', 'all'); // all | individual | institutional

        $query = Commission::with('items.product')->orderBy('created_at', 'desc');
        if (in_array($type, ['individual', 'institutional'])) {
            $query->where('client_type', $type);
        }
        $commissions = $query->get();

        $counts = [
            'all'           => Commission::count(),
            'individual'    => Commission::where('client_type', 'individual')->count(),
            'institutional' => Commission::where('client_type', 'institutional')->count(),
        ];

        return view('commissions.index', compact('commissions', 'type', 'counts'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $sites    = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        return view('commissions.create', compact('products', 'sites'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'customer_name'      => 'required|string|max:255',
            'client_type'        => 'nullable|string|max:50',
            'order_type'         => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'needed_by_date'     => 'nullable|date',
            'deposit_amount'     => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'design_description' => 'nullable|string',
            'site_id'            => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'items'              => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.product_id' => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.quantity'   => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.price'      => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        try {
            DB::beginTransaction();

            $total = 0;
            foreach ($validated['items'] as $it) {
                $total += $it['quantity'] * $it['price'];
            }
            $deposit = $validated['deposit_amount'] ?? 0;

            // single-branch: resolve a real site so the NOT NULL site_id is satisfied
            $defaultSiteId = $validated['site_id'] ?? (auth()->user()->site_id ?? Site::query()->value('id'));

            $commission = Commission::create([
                'user_id'            => auth()->id(),
                'site_id'            => $defaultSiteId,
                'customer_name'      => $validated['customer_name'],
                'client_type'        => $validated['client_type'] ?? 'individual',
                'order_type'         => $validated['order_type'] ?? null,
                'design_description' => $validated['design_description'] ?? null,
                'needed_by_date'     => $validated['needed_by_date'] ?? null,
                'total_amount'       => $total,
                'deposit_amount'     => $deposit,
                'status'             => $deposit > 0 ? 'Confirmed' : 'Inquiry',
            ]);

            foreach ($validated['items'] as $it) {
                OrderItem::create([
                    'order_id'   => $commission->id,
                    'product_id' => $it['product_id'],
                    'quantity'   => $it['quantity'],
                    'price'      => $it['price'],
                ]);
            }

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('commissions.show', $commission)
                ->with('success', 'Commission created successfully.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Commission create failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Failed to save commission: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function show(Commission $commission)
    {
        $commission->load(['items.product', 'site']);
        return view('commissions.show', compact('commission'));
    }

    public function updateStatus(Request $request, Commission $commission)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'status' => 'required|in:' . implode(',', Commission::STAGES),
        ]);
        $commission->update(['status' => $validated['status']]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Status updated to ' . $validated['status'] . '.');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Commission;
use App\Models\OrderItem;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class CommissionController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $type = $request->input('type', 'all'); // all | individual | institutional

        $query = Commission::with('items.product')->orderBy('created_at', 'desc');
        if (in_array($type, ['individual', 'institutional'])) {
            $query->where('client_type', $type);
        }
        $commissions = $query->get();

        $counts = [
            'all'           => Commission::count(),
            'individual'    => Commission::where('client_type', 'individual')->count(),
            'institutional' => Commission::where('client_type', 'institutional')->count(),
        ];

        return view('commissions.index', compact('commissions', 'type', 'counts'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')->orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $sites    = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        return view('commissions.create', compact('products', 'sites'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        // drop blank product rows before validating (lets fully-custom orders through)
        $request->merge([
            'items' => array_values(array_filter((array) $request->input('items', []), function ($it) {
                return is_array($it) && !empty($it['product_id']);
            })),
        ]);

        $validated = $request->validate([
            'customer_name'      => 'required|string|max:255',
            'client_type'        => 'nullable|string|max:50',
            'order_type'         => 'nullable|string|max:255',
            'needed_by_date'     => 'nullable|date',
            'deposit_amount'     => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'custom_amount'      => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'design_description' => 'nullable|string',
            'site_id'            => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'items'              => 'nullable|array',
            'items.*.product_id' => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.quantity'   => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'items.*.price'      => 'required|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        $items        = $validated['items'] ?? [];
        $customAmount = $validated['custom_amount'] ?? 0;

        if (count($items) === 0 && $customAmount <= 0) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Add at least one product or enter a custom order amount.');
        }

        try {
            DB::beginTransaction();

            $total = 0;
            foreach ($items as $it) {
                $total += $it['quantity'] * $it['price'];
            }
            $total += $customAmount;   // custom cake with no catalog product

            $deposit = $validated['deposit_amount'] ?? 0;

            // single-branch: resolve a real site so the NOT NULL site_id is satisfied
            $defaultSiteId = $validated['site_id'] ?? (auth()->user()->site_id ?? Site::query()->value('id'));

            $commission = Commission::create([
                'user_id'            => auth()->id(),
                'site_id'            => $defaultSiteId,
                'customer_name'      => $validated['customer_name'],
                'client_type'        => $validated['client_type'] ?? 'individual',
                'order_type'         => $validated['order_type'] ?? null,
                'design_description' => $validated['design_description'] ?? null,
                'needed_by_date'     => $validated['needed_by_date'] ?? null,
                'total_amount'       => $total,
                'deposit_amount'     => $deposit,
                'status'             => $deposit > 0 ? 'Confirmed' : 'Inquiry',
            ]);

            foreach ($items as $it) {
                OrderItem::create([
                    'order_id'   => $commission->id,
                    'product_id' => $it['product_id'],
                    'quantity'   => $it['quantity'],
                    'price'      => $it['price'],
                ]);
            }

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('commissions.show', $commission)
                ->with('success', 'Commission created successfully.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Commission create failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Failed to save commission: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function show(Commission $commission)
    {
        $commission->load(['items.product', 'site']);
        return view('commissions.show', compact('commission'));
    }

    public function updateStatus(Request $request, Commission $commission)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'status' => 'required|in:' . implode(',', Commission::STAGES),
        ]);
        if ($commission->completed_at && \App\Models\PeriodClosure::isClosed($commission->completed_at)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This commission was completed in a closed month and can no longer be changed.');
        }
        $commission->update([
            'status'       => $validated['status'],
            'completed_at' => $validated['status'] === 'Completed' ? ($commission->completed_at ?? now()) : null,
        ]);
        if ($validated['status'] === 'Completed') {
            \App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::record('order.completed', $commission, ['total' => (string) $commission->total_amount]);
        }

        return back()->with('success', 'Status updated to ' . $validated['status'] . '.');

    public function show(Commission $commission)

    public function photo(Commission $commission)

            return back()->with('error', 'This order was completed in a closed month and can no longer be changed.');

        $commission->update([
            'status'       => $validated['status'],
            'completed_at' => $validated['status'] === 'Completed' ? ($commission->completed_at ?? now()) : null,
        ]);
        if ($validated['status'] === 'Completed') {
            FinancialAuditLog::record('order.completed', $commission, ['total' => (string) $commission->total_amount]);
        }

        return back()->with('success', 'Status updated to ' . $validated['status'] . '.');
    }
}
