===== app/Http/Controllers/DashboardController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Batch;
use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\Product;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

class DashboardController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        //  This week's batches (Mon-Sun) 
        $weekStart = Carbon::now()->startOfWeek()->toDateString();
        $weekEnd   = Carbon::now()->endOfWeek()->toDateString();

        $weekBatches = Batch::with('items')
            ->whereBetween('batch_date', [$weekStart, $weekEnd])
            ->get();

        $batchSent      = $weekBatches->sum(fn ($b) => $b->totalSent());
        $returnedQty    = $weekBatches->sum(fn ($b) => $b->totalReturned());
        $netSoldQty     = $weekBatches->sum(fn ($b) => $b->totalNetSold());
        $netSoldRevenue = $weekBatches->sum(fn ($b) => $b->totalRevenue());

        // Total batches ever dispatched (sent + reconciled)
        $totalBatches = Batch::count();

        //  Inventory (raw materials) 
        $inventories = Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get();
        $lowCount    = $inventories->filter(fn ($i) => $i->isLowStock())->count();

        // lowest-stock first, show up to 6
        $stockLevels = $inventories
            ->sortBy(fn ($i) => $i->minimum_stock > 0 ? $i->usableQuantity() / $i->minimum_stock : 999)
            ->take(6)
            ->values();

        //  Finished products 
        $finishedStock = (int) Product::sum('stock_quantity');
        $totalProducts = Product::count();

        //  Latest batch (for reconciliation card) 
        $latestBatch = Batch::with(['items.product', 'sourceSite', 'destinationSite'])
            ->orderBy('batch_date', 'desc')
            ->orderBy('id', 'desc')
            ->first();

        //  Can Still Bake (limiting-ingredient math from recipes) 
        $canBake = [];
        foreach (Product::with('materials')->where('status', 'active')->get() as $product) {
            if ($product->materials->isEmpty()) {
                continue;
            }
            $caps = [];
            foreach ($product->materials as $mat) {
                $need = (float) $mat->pivot->quantity_used;
                if ($need > 0) {
                    $caps[] = (int) floor($mat->quantity_on_hand / $need);
                }
            }
            if (! empty($caps)) {
                $canBake[$product->product_name] = min($caps);
            }
        }
        arsort($canBake);
        $canBake = array_slice($canBake, 0, 5, true);
        $maxBake = ! empty($canBake) ? max(array_values($canBake)) : 0;

        //  Recent stock movements (ingredient audit trail) 
        $movements = InventoryLog::with('inventory')
            ->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')
            ->take(6)
            ->get();

        return view('dashboard', compact(
            'batchSent', 'totalBatches', 'returnedQty', 'netSoldQty', 'netSoldRevenue',
            'lowCount', 'stockLevels', 'finishedStock', 'totalProducts',
            'latestBatch', 'canBake', 'maxBake', 'movements'
        ));
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/ExpenseController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Expense;
use App\Models\FinancialAuditLog;
use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\PeriodClosure;
use App\Services\InventoryFifoService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

/**
 * Expense ledger. There is intentionally NO edit and NO delete:
 * mistakes are voided (with a reason) and re-entered, and every action is audit-logged.
 */
class ExpenseController extends Controller
{
    private const CATEGORIES = 'ingredients,packaging,utilities,rent,transport,labor,maintenance,other';

    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'from' => 'nullable|date', 'to' => 'nullable|date', 'category' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);

        $from = Carbon::parse($request->input('from', now()->startOfMonth()->toDateString()))->startOfDay();
        $to   = Carbon::parse($request->input('to', now()->endOfMonth()->toDateString()))->endOfDay();

        $query = Expense::with(['inventoryItem', 'creator', 'voider'])
            ->whereBetween('expense_date', [$from->toDateString(), $to->toDateString()]);
        if ($request->filled('category')) {
            $query->where('category', $request->category);
        }

        $expenses = $query->orderByDesc('expense_date')->orderByDesc('id')->get();
        $activeTotal = $expenses->whereNull('voided_at')->sum('amount');
        $voidedTotal = $expenses->whereNotNull('voided_at')->sum('amount');

        $inventoryItems = Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get();
        $closedMonths = PeriodClosure::pluck('period')->map(fn ($d) => $d->format('Y-m'))->all();

        return view('expenses.index', [
            'expenses' => $expenses, 'inventoryItems' => $inventoryItems,
            'from' => $from->toDateString(), 'to' => $to->toDateString(),
            'category' => $request->category, 'activeTotal' => $activeTotal,
            'voidedTotal' => $voidedTotal, 'closedMonths' => $closedMonths,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        // Restocking only applies to Ingredients; ignore the stock fields for any other category.
        if ($request->input('category') !== 'ingredients') {
            $request->merge(['inventory_id' => null, 'quantity' => null]);
        }

        $v = $request->validate([
            'expense_date' => 'required|date|before_or_equal:today',
            'category'     => 'required|string|in:' . self::CATEGORIES,
            'description'  => 'required|string|max:255',
            'amount'       => 'required|numeric|min:0.01|max:9999999.99',
            'inventory_id' => 'nullable|exists:inventory,id|required_with:quantity',
            'quantity'     => 'nullable|integer|min:1|required_with:inventory_id',
            'receipt'      => 'nullable|file|mimes:jpg,jpeg,png,pdf|max:4096',
        ]);

        if (PeriodClosure::isClosed($v['expense_date'])) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error',
                Carbon::parse($v['expense_date'])->format('F Y') . ' is closed. Record this in the current open month instead.');
        }

        DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $v) {
            $data = collect($v)->except('receipt')->all();
            $data['created_by'] = auth()->id();
            if ($request->hasFile('receipt')) {
                $data['receipt_path'] = $request->file('receipt')->store('receipts', 'local');
            }

            $expense = Expense::create($data);

            // Linked ingredient purchase: stock the item in and write it to the inventory history
            if (!empty($data['inventory_id']) && !empty($data['quantity'])) {
                Inventory::where('id', $data['inventory_id'])->increment('quantity_on_hand', $data['quantity']);
                InventoryLog::create([
                    'inventory_id' => $data['inventory_id'],
                    'type'         => 'received',
                    'quantity'     => $data['quantity'],
                    'notes'        => '[EXPENSE-' . $expense->id . '] Purchased via Expenses: ' . $expense->description,
                    'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
                ]);

                // FIFO: record this purchase as a cost lot (oldest-first costing)
                InventoryFifoService::receive(
                    $data['inventory_id'],
                    $data['quantity'],
                    $data['quantity'] > 0 ? round($expense->amount / $data['quantity'], 2) : 0,
                    $expense->expense_date,
                    $expense->id
                );
            }

            FinancialAuditLog::record('expense.recorded', $expense, [
                'date' => $expense->expense_date->toDateString(), 'category' => $expense->category,
                'amount' => (string) $expense->amount, 'description' => $expense->description,
                'restocked' => !empty($data['inventory_id']) ? ($data['quantity'] . ' x inventory#' . $data['inventory_id']) : null,
                'has_receipt' => isset($data['receipt_path']),
            ]);
        });

        return redirect()->route('expenses.index')->with('success', 'Expense recorded.');
    }

    public function void(Request $request, Expense $expense)
    {
        $request->validate(['void_reason' => 'required|string|min:5|max:255']);

        if (PeriodClosure::isClosed($expense->expense_date)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This expense belongs to a closed month and can no longer be voided.');
        }

        $result = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $expense) {
            $locked = Expense::lockForUpdate()->find($expense->id);
            if ($locked->isVoided()) {
                return 'already';
            }

            $locked->update([
                'voided_at' => now(), 'voided_by' => auth()->id(), 'void_reason' => $request->void_reason,
            ]);

            // Undo the restock this expense caused (never below zero)
            $note = null;
            if ($locked->inventory_id && $locked->quantity) {
                $inv = Inventory::lockForUpdate()->find($locked->inventory_id);
                if ($inv) {
                    $undo = min((int) $locked->quantity, (int) $inv->quantity_on_hand);
                    $inv->decrement('quantity_on_hand', $undo);
                    $note = "stock reversed by {$undo} of {$locked->quantity}";
                    InventoryLog::create([
                        'inventory_id' => $inv->id,
                        'type'         => 'adjustment',
                        'quantity'     => $undo,
                        'notes'        => '[EXPENSE-VOID-' . $locked->id . '] Expense voided, stock removed: ' . $locked->description,
                        'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
                    ]);

                    // FIFO: reverse this purchase's cost lot
                    InventoryFifoService::reverseExpense($locked->id, $undo);
                }
            }

            FinancialAuditLog::record('expense.voided', $locked, [
                'amount' => (string) $locked->amount, 'description' => $locked->description,
                'reason' => $request->void_reason, 'inventory' => $note,
            ]);
            return 'ok';
        });

        return $result === 'already'
            ? back()->with('error', 'That expense was already voided.')
            : back()->with('success', 'Expense voided. It stays on record but no longer counts toward totals.');
    }

    public function receipt(Expense $expense)
    {
        abort_unless($expense->receipt_path && Storage::disk('local')->exists($expense->receipt_path), 404);
        FinancialAuditLog::record('expense.receipt_viewed', $expense);

        return Storage::disk('local')->response($expense->receipt_path);
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/ExpenseController.php.bak-fifo2 =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Expense;
use App\Models\FinancialAuditLog;
use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\PeriodClosure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

/**
 * Expense ledger. There is intentionally NO edit and NO delete:
 * mistakes are voided (with a reason) and re-entered, and every action is audit-logged.
 */
class ExpenseController extends Controller
{
    private const CATEGORIES = 'ingredients,packaging,utilities,rent,transport,labor,maintenance,other';

    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $request->validate([
            'from' => 'nullable|date', 'to' => 'nullable|date', 'category' => 'nullable|string',
        ]);

        $from = Carbon::parse($request->input('from', now()->startOfMonth()->toDateString()))->startOfDay();
        $to   = Carbon::parse($request->input('to', now()->endOfMonth()->toDateString()))->endOfDay();

        $query = Expense::with(['inventoryItem', 'creator', 'voider'])
            ->whereBetween('expense_date', [$from->toDateString(), $to->toDateString()]);
        if ($request->filled('category')) {
            $query->where('category', $request->category);
        }

        $expenses = $query->orderByDesc('expense_date')->orderByDesc('id')->get();
        $activeTotal = $expenses->whereNull('voided_at')->sum('amount');
        $voidedTotal = $expenses->whereNotNull('voided_at')->sum('amount');

        $inventoryItems = Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get();
        $closedMonths = PeriodClosure::pluck('period')->map(fn ($d) => $d->format('Y-m'))->all();

        return view('expenses.index', [
            'expenses' => $expenses, 'inventoryItems' => $inventoryItems,
            'from' => $from->toDateString(), 'to' => $to->toDateString(),
            'category' => $request->category, 'activeTotal' => $activeTotal,
            'voidedTotal' => $voidedTotal, 'closedMonths' => $closedMonths,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        // Restocking only applies to Ingredients; ignore the stock fields for any other category.
        if ($request->input('category') !== 'ingredients') {
            $request->merge(['inventory_id' => null, 'quantity' => null]);
        }

        $v = $request->validate([
            'expense_date' => 'required|date|before_or_equal:today',
            'category'     => 'required|string|in:' . self::CATEGORIES,
            'description'  => 'required|string|max:255',
            'amount'       => 'required|numeric|min:0.01|max:9999999.99',
            'inventory_id' => 'nullable|exists:inventory,id|required_with:quantity',
            'quantity'     => 'nullable|integer|min:1|required_with:inventory_id',
            'receipt'      => 'nullable|file|mimes:jpg,jpeg,png,pdf|max:4096',
        ]);

        if (PeriodClosure::isClosed($v['expense_date'])) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error',
                Carbon::parse($v['expense_date'])->format('F Y') . ' is closed. Record this in the current open month instead.');
        }

        DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $v) {
            $data = collect($v)->except('receipt')->all();
            $data['created_by'] = auth()->id();
            if ($request->hasFile('receipt')) {
                $data['receipt_path'] = $request->file('receipt')->store('receipts', 'local');
            }

            $expense = Expense::create($data);

            // Linked ingredient purchase: stock the item in and write it to the inventory history
            if (!empty($data['inventory_id']) && !empty($data['quantity'])) {
                Inventory::where('id', $data['inventory_id'])->increment('quantity_on_hand', $data['quantity']);
                InventoryLog::create([
                    'inventory_id' => $data['inventory_id'],
                    'type'         => 'received',
                    'quantity'     => $data['quantity'],
                    'notes'        => '[EXPENSE-' . $expense->id . '] Purchased via Expenses: ' . $expense->description,
                    'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
                ]);
            }
             // FIFO: record this purchase as a cost lot (oldest-first costing)
                \App\Services\InventoryFifoService::receive(
                    $data['inventory_id'],
                    $data['quantity'],
                    $data['quantity'] > 0 ? round($expense->amount / $data['quantity'], 2) : 0,
                    $expense->expense_date,
                    $expense->id
                );
            FinancialAuditLog::record('expense.recorded', $expense, [
                'date' => $expense->expense_date->toDateString(), 'category' => $expense->category,
                'amount' => (string) $expense->amount, 'description' => $expense->description,
                'restocked' => !empty($data['inventory_id']) ? ($data['quantity'] . ' x inventory#' . $data['inventory_id']) : null,
                'has_receipt' => isset($data['receipt_path']),
            ]);
        });

        return redirect()->route('expenses.index')->with('success', 'Expense recorded.');
    }

    public function void(Request $request, Expense $expense)
    {
        $request->validate(['void_reason' => 'required|string|min:5|max:255']);

        if (PeriodClosure::isClosed($expense->expense_date)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'This expense belongs to a closed month and can no longer be voided.');
        }

        $result = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $expense) {
            $locked = Expense::lockForUpdate()->find($expense->id);
            if ($locked->isVoided()) {
                return 'already';
            }

            $locked->update([
                'voided_at' => now(), 'voided_by' => auth()->id(), 'void_reason' => $request->void_reason,
            ]);

            // Undo the restock this expense caused (never below zero)
            $note = null;
            if ($locked->inventory_id && $locked->quantity) {
                $inv = Inventory::lockForUpdate()->find($locked->inventory_id);
                if ($inv) {
                    $undo = min((int) $locked->quantity, (int) $inv->quantity_on_hand);
                    $inv->decrement('quantity_on_hand', $undo);
                    $note = "stock reversed by {$undo} of {$locked->quantity}";
                    InventoryLog::create([
                        'inventory_id' => $inv->id,
                        'type'         => 'adjustment',
                        'quantity'     => $undo,
                        'notes'        => '[EXPENSE-VOID-' . $locked->id . '] Expense voided, stock removed: ' . $locked->description,
                        'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
                    ]);
                }
            }

            FinancialAuditLog::record('expense.voided', $locked, [
                'amount' => (string) $locked->amount, 'description' => $locked->description,
                'reason' => $request->void_reason, 'inventory' => $note,
            ]);
            return 'ok';
        });

        return $result === 'already'
            ? back()->with('error', 'That expense was already voided.')
            : back()->with('success', 'Expense voided. It stays on record but no longer counts toward totals.');
    }

    public function receipt(Expense $expense)
    {
        abort_unless($expense->receipt_path && Storage::disk('local')->exists($expense->receipt_path), 404);
        FinancialAuditLog::record('expense.receipt_viewed', $expense);

        return Storage::disk('local')->response($expense->receipt_path);
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/FinancialStatementController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\FinancialAuditLog;
use App\Models\PeriodClosure;
use App\Services\FinancialStatementService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

/**
 * The Financial Statement is READ-ONLY. It is computed live from the ledger
 * (or verified against the frozen snapshot once a month is closed).
 */
class FinancialStatementController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request, FinancialStatementService $svc)
    {
        $request->validate(['from' => 'nullable|date', 'to' => 'nullable|date|after_or_equal:from']);

        $from = Carbon::parse($request->input('from', now()->startOfMonth()->toDateString()))->startOfDay();
        $to   = Carbon::parse($request->input('to', now()->endOfMonth()->toDateString()))->endOfDay();

        $s = $svc->build($from, $to);

        // Is the selected range exactly one calendar month? Then it can be closed / verified.
        $isWholeMonth = $from->isSameDay($from->copy()->startOfMonth()) && $to->isSameDay($from->copy()->endOfMonth());
        $closure = $isWholeMonth ? PeriodClosure::whereDate('period', $from->toDateString())->first() : null;

        $integrity = null;
        if ($closure) {
            $integrity = [
                'hash_ok'    => $closure->hashIsValid(),
                'figures_ok' => abs($closure->net_income - $s['netIncome']) < 0.005
                             && abs($closure->revenue - $s['revenue']) < 0.005
                             && abs($closure->cogs - $s['cogs']) < 0.005
                             && abs($closure->opex - $s['opex']) < 0.005,
            ];
        }

        $canClose = $isWholeMonth && !$closure && $to->lt(now()->startOfDay()) && auth()->user()->isOwner();

        return view('financial-statement.index', [
            's' => $s, 'from' => $from, 'to' => $to, 'closure' => $closure,
            'integrity' => $integrity, 'isWholeMonth' => $isWholeMonth, 'canClose' => $canClose,
            'closures' => PeriodClosure::orderByDesc('period')->limit(12)->get(),
            'preparedBy' => trim(auth()->user()->first_name . ' ' . auth()->user()->last_name),
        ]);
    }

    /** Owner-only. Freezes a finished month permanently. */
    public function close(Request $request, FinancialStatementService $svc)
    {
        abort_unless(auth()->user()->isOwner(), 403, 'Only the Owner can close a month.');
        $request->validate(['month' => ['required', 'regex:/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/']]);

        $start = Carbon::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $request->month . '-01')->startOfDay();
        $end   = $start->copy()->endOfMonth();

        if (!$end->lt(now()->startOfDay())) {
            return back()->with('error', 'A month can only be closed after it has ended.');
        }
        if (PeriodClosure::isClosed($start)) {
            return back()->with('error', $start->format('F Y') . ' is already closed.');
        }

        try {
            DB::transaction(function () use ($svc, $start, $end) {
                $s    = $svc->build($start, $end);
                $prev = PeriodClosure::orderByDesc('period')->first();
                $at   = now()->format('Y-m-d H:i:s');
                $name = trim(auth()->user()->first_name . ' ' . auth()->user()->last_name);

                $closure = PeriodClosure::create([
                    'period' => $start->toDateString(),
                    'revenue' => $s['revenue'], 'cogs' => $s['cogs'], 'opex' => $s['opex'], 'net_income' => $s['netIncome'],
                    'closed_by' => auth()->id(), 'closed_by_name' => $name, 'closed_at' => $at,
                    'prev_hash' => $prev?->hash,
                    'hash' => PeriodClosure::computeHash($prev?->hash, $start->toDateString(),
                        $s['revenue'], $s['cogs'], $s['opex'], $s['netIncome'], $at, $name),
                ]);

                FinancialAuditLog::record('period.closed', $closure, [
                    'period' => $start->format('Y-m'), 'revenue' => $s['revenue'], 'cogs' => $s['cogs'],
                    'opex' => $s['opex'], 'net_income' => $s['netIncome'],
                ]);
            });
        } catch (\Illuminate\Database\QueryException $e) {
            return back()->with('error', 'That month was just closed by someone else.');
        }

        return redirect()->route('financial-statement.index', [
            'from' => $start->toDateString(), 'to' => $end->toDateString(),
        ])->with('success', $start->format('F Y') . ' is now closed and locked.');
    }

    /** Read-only audit trail. */
    public function auditLog(Request $request)
    {
        $q = FinancialAuditLog::query()->orderByDesc('id');
        if ($request->filled('action')) {
            $q->where('action', 'like', $request->action . '%');
        }

        $per  = 50;
        $page = max(1, (int) $request->input('page', 1));
        $rows = $q->skip(($page - 1) * $per)->take($per + 1)->get();

        return view('financial-statement.audit-log', [
            'logs' => $rows->take($per), 'hasMore' => $rows->count() > $per,
            'page' => $page, 'action' => $request->action,
        ]);
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/Inventory/ValuationController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Inventory;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Services\InventoryFifoService;

class ValuationController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $rows = [];
        $grand = 0.0;

        foreach (Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get() as $inv) {
            $lots      = InventoryFifoService::lots($inv->id);
            $remaining = round(array_sum(array_column($lots, 'remaining')), 2);
            $value     = round(array_sum(array_column($lots, 'value')), 2);
            $grand    += $value;

            $rows[] = [
                'inv'       => $inv,
                'lots'      => $lots,
                'remaining' => $remaining,
                'onHand'    => (float) $inv->quantity_on_hand,
                'avgCost'   => $remaining > 0 ? round($value / $remaining, 2) : 0.0,
                'value'     => $value,
            ];
        }

        return view('inventory.valuation', [
            'rows'  => $rows,
            'grand' => round($grand, 2),
        ]);
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/InventoryController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class InventoryController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $view = $request->get('view') === 'archived' ? 'archived' : 'active';

        $base = Inventory::query();
        if ($request->search) {
            $base->where('item_name', 'like', '%' . $request->search . '%');
        }

        $activeCount   = (clone $base)->whereNull('archived_at')->count();
        $archivedCount = (clone $base)->whereNotNull('archived_at')->count();

        $q = clone $base;
        if ($view === 'archived') { $q->whereNotNull('archived_at'); }
        else { $q->whereNull('archived_at'); }

        $inventories = $q->orderBy('id')->get();
        $lowStock = $inventories->filter(fn($i) => $i->isLowStock())->count();
        $damaged  = $inventories->sum('quantity_damaged');

        return view('inventory.index', compact('inventories', 'lowStock', 'damaged', 'view', 'activeCount', 'archivedCount'));
    }

    public function lowStockReport()
    {
        $items = Inventory::whereNull('archived_at')
            ->orderBy('item_name')
            ->get()
            ->filter(function ($i) {
                $on  = (float) $i->quantity_on_hand;
                $min = (float) $i->minimum_stock;
                return $on <= $min * 1.5; // low / critical / out
            })
            ->sortBy(function ($i) {
                $min = (float) $i->minimum_stock;
                return $min > 0 ? ((float) $i->quantity_on_hand) / $min : 999;
            })
            ->values();

        return view('inventory.low-stock-report', compact('items'));
    }
    public function create()
    {
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        $categories = ['Baking Essentials', 'Dairy & Eggs', 'Flavoring & Fillings', 'Packaging', 'Coffee & Beverage', 'Other'];
        $units = ['g', 'kg', 'ml', 'liters', 'pcs', 'dozen', 'pack'];
        return view('inventory.create', compact('sites', 'categories', 'units'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'item_name'        => 'required|string|max:255',
            'category'         => 'required|string|max:255',
            'unit'             => 'required|string|max:50',
            'quantity_on_hand' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'quantity_damaged' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'minimum_stock'    => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'site_id'          => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
            'notes'            => 'nullable|string',
            'unit_cost'        => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
        ]);

        $inventory = Inventory::create([
            'item_name'        => $validated['item_name'],
            'category'         => $validated['category'],
            'unit'             => $validated['unit'],
            'quantity_on_hand' => $validated['quantity_on_hand'],
            'quantity_damaged' => $validated['quantity_damaged'] ?? 0,
            'minimum_stock'    => $validated['minimum_stock'],
            'site_id'          => $validated['site_id'] ?? null,
            'notes'            => $validated['notes'] ?? null,
        ]);

        InventoryLog::create([
            'inventory_id' => $inventory->id,
            'type'         => 'adjustment',
            'quantity'     => $inventory->quantity_on_hand,
            'ref_note'     => 'INITIAL-STOCK',
            'notes'        => 'Initial inventory record created',
            'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
        ]);

        // FIFO: opening lot for the starting quantity, at its unit cost
        if ((float) $inventory->quantity_on_hand > 0) {
            \App\Services\InventoryFifoService::receive(
                $inventory->id,
                $inventory->quantity_on_hand,
                $validated['unit_cost'] ?? 0,
                now()->toDateString(),
                null
            );
        }
        return redirect()->route('inventory.index')->with('success', 'Inventory item added successfully.');
    }

    public function show(Inventory $inventory)
    {
        $inventory->load(['site', 'logs.user']);
        return view('inventory.show', compact('inventory'));
    }

    public function adjust(Request $request, Inventory $inventory)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'type'     => 'required|in:used,damaged,adjustment',
            'quantity' => 'required|numeric|min:0.01',
            'notes'    => 'nullable|string',
        ]);

        if ($validated['type'] === 'used') {
            $inventory->decrement('quantity_on_hand', $validated['quantity']);
        } elseif ($validated['type'] === 'damaged') {
            $inventory->decrement('quantity_on_hand', $validated['quantity']);
            $inventory->increment('quantity_damaged', $validated['quantity']);
        } else {
            $inventory->increment('quantity_on_hand', $validated['quantity']);
        }

        InventoryLog::create([
            'inventory_id' => $inventory->id,
            'type'         => $validated['type'],
            'quantity'     => $validated['quantity'],
            'notes'        => $validated['notes'] ?? null,
            'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Inventory adjusted successfully.');
    }

    public function edit(Inventory $inventory)
    {
        $sites = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        $categories = ['Baking Essentials', 'Dairy & Eggs', 'Flavoring & Fillings', 'Packaging', 'Coffee & Beverage', 'Other'];
        $units = ['g', 'kg', 'ml', 'liters', 'pcs', 'dozen', 'pack'];
        return view('inventory.edit', compact('inventory', 'sites', 'categories', 'units'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Inventory $inventory)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'item_name'     => 'required|string|max:255',
            'category'      => 'required|string|max:255',
            'unit'          => 'required|string|max:50',
            'quantity_on_hand' => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'minimum_stock'    => 'required|numeric|min:0',
            'site_id'          => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
        ]);

        $oldQty = (float) $inventory->quantity_on_hand;
        $newQty = isset($validated['quantity_on_hand']) ? (float) $validated['quantity_on_hand'] : $oldQty;

        $inventory->update($validated);

        if ($oldQty != $newQty) {
            InventoryLog::create([
                'inventory_id' => $inventory->id,
                'type'         => 'adjustment',
                'quantity'     => abs($newQty - $oldQty),
                'reference'    => 'EDIT-CORRECTION',
                'notes'        => 'Quantity corrected via Edit: ' . $oldQty . ' -> ' . $newQty,
                'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
            ]);
        }

        return redirect()->route('inventory.index')->with('success', 'Item updated successfully.');
    }

    public function archive(Inventory $inventory)
    {
        $inventory->archived_at = now();
        $inventory->save();
        return redirect()->route('inventory.index')->with('success', $inventory->item_name . ' archived.');
    }

    public function restore(Inventory $inventory)
    {
        $inventory->archived_at = null;
        $inventory->save();
        return redirect()->route('inventory.index', ['view' => 'archived'])->with('success', $inventory->item_name . ' restored.');
    }

    public function destroy(Inventory $inventory)
    {
        if (auth()->user()->role !== 'Owner') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Only the Owner can permanently delete records.');
        }
        $inventory->delete();
        return redirect()->route('inventory.index', ['view' => 'archived'])->with('success', 'Item permanently deleted.');
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/InventoryController.php.bak =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class InventoryController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $view = $request->get('view') === 'archived' ? 'archived' : 'active';

        $base = Inventory::query();
        if ($request->search) {
            $base->where('item_name', 'like', '%' . $request->search . '%');
        }

        $activeCount   = (clone $base)->whereNull('archived_at')->count();
        $archivedCount = (clone $base)->whereNotNull('archived_at')->count();

        $q = clone $base;
        if ($view === 'archived') { $q->whereNotNull('archived_at'); }
        else { $q->whereNull('archived_at'); }

        $inventories = $q->latest()->get();
        $lowStock = $inventories->filter(fn($i) => $i->isLowStock())->count();
        $damaged  = $inventories->sum('quantity_damaged');

        return view('inventory.index', compact('inventories', 'lowStock', 'damaged', 'view', 'activeCount', 'archivedCount'));
    }

===== app/Http/Controllers/OrderController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Order;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class OrderController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $orders = Order::with('site', 'items', 'user')->latest()->get();
        return view('orders.index', compact('orders'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $products = Product::orderBy('product_name')->get();
        $sites    = Site::orderBy('site_name')->get();
        return view('orders.create', compact('products', 'sites'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $validated = $request->validate([
            'site_id'            => 'required|exists:sites,id',
            'customer_name'      => 'nullable|string|max:100',
            'order_type'         => 'required|in:Walk-in,Customized,Institutional',
            'design_description' => 'required_if:order_type,Customized|nullable|string',
            'needed_by_date'     => 'required_if:order_type,Customized|nullable|date',
            'deposit_amount'     => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
            'items'              => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.product_id' => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.quantity'   => 'required|integer|min:1',
        ]);

        $total = 0;
        $lineItems = [];

        foreach ($validated['items'] as $item) {
            $product = Product::findOrFail($item['product_id']);
            $lineTotal = $product->price * $item['quantity'];
            $total += $lineTotal;

            $lineItems[] = [
                'product_id' => $product->id,
                'quantity'   => $item['quantity'],
                'price'      => $product->price,
            ];
        }

        $order = Order::create([
            'user_id'            => auth()->id(),
            'site_id'            => $validated['site_id'],
            'customer_name'      => $validated['customer_name'] ?? 'Walk-in',
            'order_type'         => $validated['order_type'],
            'design_description' => $validated['design_description'] ?? null,
            'needed_by_date'     => $validated['needed_by_date'] ?? null,
            'total_amount'       => $total,
            'deposit_amount'     => $validated['deposit_amount'] ?? 0,
            'status'             => 'Pending',
        ]);

        foreach ($lineItems as $line) {
            $order->items()->create($line);

            // Deduct from the product's own finished-goods stock.
            // NOTE: this only deducts at the Product level for now.
            // Raw-material-level deduction (per recipe) is a planned
            // follow-up once Inventory and RawMaterial are unified.
            Product::where('id', $line['product_id'])
                ->decrement('stock_quantity', $line['quantity']);
        }

        return redirect()->route('orders.show', $order)
            ->with('success', 'Order placed successfully.');
    }

    public function show(Order $order)
    {
        $order->load('site', 'items.product', 'user');
        return view('orders.show', compact('order'));
    }

    public function destroy(Order $order)
    {
        if ($order->status !== 'Pending') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Only pending orders can be cancelled.');
        }
        $order->items()->delete();
        $order->delete();
        return redirect()->route('orders.index')->with('success', 'Order cancelled.');
    }
    public function updateStatus(Request $request, Order $order)
{
    $request->validate(['status' => 'required|in:Pending,In Production,Ready,Completed']);
    if ($order->completed_at && \App\Models\PeriodClosure::isClosed($order->completed_at)) {
        return back()->with('error', 'This order was completed in a closed month and can no longer be changed.');
    }
    $order->update([
        'status'       => $request->status,
        'completed_at' => $request->status === 'Completed' ? ($order->completed_at ?? now()) : null,
    ]);
    if ($request->status === 'Completed') {
        \App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::record('order.completed', $order, ['total' => (string) $order->total_amount]);
    }
    return back()->with('success', 'Order status updated.');
}
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/PosController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Sale;
use App\Models\SalePayment;
use App\Models\DayClosure;
use App\Models\Site;
use App\Models\FinancialAuditLog;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

/**
 * POS â€” counter sale of FINISHED products (walk-in).
 * Deducts finished stock (Product.stock_quantity) only. Raw materials are NOT
 * touched here: they were already consumed when the product was produced.
 * A sale is a ledger entry â€” it is voided (with a reason), never deleted.
 */
class PosController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $products = Product::where('status', 'active')
            ->orderBy('product_name')
            ->get(['id', 'product_name', 'category', 'price', 'stock_quantity', 'image_path']);

        $recent = Sale::with('cashier')
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->limit(8)
            ->get();

        return view('pos.index', compact('products', 'recent'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            'items'                => 'required|array|min:1',
            'items.*.product_id'   => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'items.*.quantity'     => 'required|integer|min:1',
            'payments'             => 'required|array|min:1',
            'payments.*.method'    => 'required|in:' . implode(',', SalePayment::METHODS),
            'payments.*.amount'    => 'required|numeric|min:0.01',
            'payments.*.reference' => 'nullable|string|max:100',
        ]);

        // Digital payments must carry a reference number.
        foreach ($data['payments'] as $p) {
            if ($p['method'] !== 'cash' && blank($p['reference'] ?? null)) {
                return back()->withInput()
                    ->with('error', 'A reference number is required for ' . strtoupper($p['method']) . ' payments.');
            }
        }

        try {
            $sale = DB::transaction(function () use ($data) {
                // Lock the products we are about to sell so two cashiers cannot
                // oversell the same stock at the same time.
                $ids = collect($data['items'])->pluck('product_id')->all();
                $products = Product::whereIn('id', $ids)->lockForUpdate()->get()->keyBy('id');

                $total = 0;
                $lines = [];
                foreach ($data['items'] as $row) {
                    $product = $products[$row['product_id']];
                    $qty = (int) $row['quantity'];
                    if ((float) $product->stock_quantity < $qty) {
                        throw new \RuntimeException(
                            "Not enough stock for {$product->product_name} (have {$product->stock_quantity}, need {$qty})."
                        );
                    }
                    $lineTotal = round((float) $product->price * $qty, 2);
                    $total += $lineTotal;
                    $lines[] = [$product, $qty, $lineTotal];
                }

                $paid = round(collect($data['payments'])->sum(fn ($p) => (float) $p['amount']), 2);
                if ($paid + 0.009 < $total) {
                    throw new \RuntimeException(
                        'Payments (' . number_format($paid, 2) . ') are less than the total (' . number_format($total, 2) . ').'
                    );
                }

                $siteId = auth()->user()->site_id ?? Site::query()->value('id');

                $sale = Sale::create([
                    'sale_number'  => 'S-' . now()->format('ymd') . '-' . strtoupper(substr(uniqid(), -5)),
                    'site_id'      => $siteId,
                    'cashier_id'   => auth()->id(),
                    'total'        => $total,
                    'status'       => 'completed',
                    'completed_at' => now(),
                ]);

                foreach ($lines as [$product, $qty, $lineTotal]) {
                    $sale->items()->create([
                        'product_id'   => $product->id,
                        'product_name' => $product->product_name,
                        'quantity'     => $qty,
                        'unit_price'   => $product->price,
                        'line_total'   => $lineTotal,
                    ]);
                    $product->decrement('stock_quantity', $qty); // finished stock only
                }

                foreach ($data['payments'] as $p) {
                    if ((float) $p['amount'] <= 0) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    $sale->payments()->create([
                        'method'    => $p['method'],
                        'amount'    => round((float) $p['amount'], 2),
                        'reference' => $p['method'] === 'cash' ? null : ($p['reference'] ?? null),
                    ]);
                }

                FinancialAuditLog::record('sale.recorded', $sale, [
                    'total'    => (string) $sale->total,
                    'items'    => count($lines),
                    'payments' => collect($data['payments'])
                        ->map(fn ($p) => $p['method'] . ':' . $p['amount'])->implode(', '),
                ]);

                return $sale;
            });
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', $e->getMessage());
        }

        return redirect()->route('pos.index')
            ->with('success', 'Sale ' . $sale->sale_number . ' recorded. Total: ' . number_format($sale->total, 2) . '.');
    }

    public function void(Request $request, Sale $sale)
    {
        $data = $request->validate(['reason' => 'required|string|max:255']);

        if ($sale->isVoided()) {
            return back()->with('error', 'That sale is already voided.');
        }
        if (DayClosure::isClosed($sale->completed_at)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'That sale falls on a day that is already closed. It can no longer be voided.');
        }

        DB::transaction(function () use ($sale, $data) {
            foreach ($sale->items as $item) {
                if ($item->product_id) {
                    $product = Product::where('id', $item->product_id)->lockForUpdate()->first();
                    $product?->increment('stock_quantity', $item->quantity); // put the stock back
                }
            }

            $sale->update([
                'status'      => 'voided',
                'voided_at'   => now(),
                'voided_by'   => auth()->id(),
                'void_reason' => $data['reason'],
            ]);

            FinancialAuditLog::record('sale.voided', $sale, [
                'reason' => $data['reason'],
                'total'  => (string) $sale->total,
            ]);
        });

        return back()->with('success', 'Sale ' . $sale->sale_number . ' voided and stock restored.');
    }
}

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

<?php

===== app/Http/Controllers/ProductController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class ProductController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $products = Product::with('materials')->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->get();
        return view('products.index', compact('products'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $inventoryItems = Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get();
        $sites = Site::all();
        return view('products.create', compact('inventoryItems', 'sites'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        try {
            $validated = $request->validate([
                'product_name'  => 'required|string|max:255|unique:products',
                'description'   => 'nullable|string|max:1000',
                'category'      => 'nullable|string|max:255',
                'price'         => 'required|numeric|min:0',
                'stock_quantity' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
                'site_id'       => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
                'image'         => 'nullable|image|max:2048',
                'recipe'        => 'nullable|array',
                'recipe.*.inventory_id' => 'numeric|exists:inventory,id',
                'recipe.*.quantity_needed' => 'numeric|min:0.01',
            ]);

            DB::beginTransaction();

            $imagePath = null;
            if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
                $imagePath = $request->file('image')->store('products', 'public');
            }

            $qtyToMake = (float) ($validated['stock_quantity'] ?? 0);

            $product = Product::create([
                'product_name'   => $validated['product_name'],
                'description'    => $validated['description'] ?? null,
                'category'       => $validated['category'] ?? null,
                'price'          => $validated['price'],
                'stock_quantity' => $qtyToMake,
                'site_id'        => $validated['site_id'] ?? null,
                'status'         => 'active',
                'image_path'     => $imagePath,
            ]);

            if (! empty($validated['recipe'])) {
                $materials = [];
                foreach ($validated['recipe'] as $recipe_item) {
                    if (empty($recipe_item['inventory_id'])) continue;

                    $inventory_id = $recipe_item['inventory_id'];
                    $perUnit      = (float) $recipe_item['quantity_needed'];
                    $inventory    = Inventory::findOrFail($inventory_id);

                    // Deduct for the whole starting batch (recipe x quantity produced now)
                    $totalNeeded = $perUnit * $qtyToMake;

                    if ($totalNeeded > 0) {
                        if ($inventory->quantity_on_hand < $totalNeeded) {
                            throw new \Exception(
                                "Insufficient {$inventory->item_name}. Available: {$inventory->quantity_on_hand}, Needed: {$totalNeeded}"
                            );
                        }

                        $inventory->update([
                            'quantity_on_hand' => $inventory->quantity_on_hand - $totalNeeded
                        ]);

                        InventoryLog::create([
                            'inventory_id' => $inventory_id,
                            'type'         => 'used',
                            'quantity'     => $totalNeeded,
                            'reference'    => 'PRODUCT-CREATE',
                            'notes'        => "Produced {$qtyToMake} x {$product->product_name} on create",
                            'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
                        ]);
                    }

                    // Store the per-unit recipe (mirror for the active recipe / Recipes page)
                    $materials[$inventory_id] = ['quantity_used' => $perUnit];
                }

                if (! empty($materials)) {
                    $product->materials()->attach($materials);
                }
            }

            DB::commit();

            $msg = $qtyToMake > 0
                ? "Product '{$product->product_name}' created and {$qtyToMake} pcs produced. Ingredients deducted from inventory."
                : "Product '{$product->product_name}' created. You can produce a batch anytime from its Recipes page.";

            return redirect()->route('products.index')->with('success', $msg);
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Product creation failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()
                ->with('error', 'Failed to create product: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function show(Product $product)
    {
        $product->load('materials');
        return view('products.show', compact('product'));
    }

    public function edit(Product $product)
    {
        $inventoryItems = Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get();
        $sites = Site::all();
        $product->load('materials');
        return view('products.edit', compact('product', 'inventoryItems', 'sites'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Product $product)
    {
        try {
            $validated = $request->validate([
                'product_name'  => 'required|string|max:255|unique:products,product_name,' . $product->id,
                'description'   => 'nullable|string|max:1000',
                'category'      => 'nullable|string|max:255',
                'price'         => 'required|numeric|min:0',
                'site_id'       => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
                'status'        => 'nullable|in:active,inactive',
                'image'         => 'nullable|image|max:2048',
            ]);

            if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
                if ($product->image_path && Storage::disk('public')->exists($product->image_path)) {
                    Storage::disk('public')->delete($product->image_path);
                }
                $validated['image_path'] = $request->file('image')->store('products', 'public');
            }

            unset($validated['image']);
            $product->update($validated);

            return redirect()->route('products.index')
                ->with('success', 'Product updated successfully.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            \Log::error('Product update failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()
                ->with('error', 'Failed to update product. Please try again.');
        }
    }

    public function destroy(Product $product)
    {
        try {
            if ($product->image_path && Storage::disk('public')->exists($product->image_path)) {
                Storage::disk('public')->delete($product->image_path);
            }
            $product->delete();
            return redirect()->route('products.index')
                ->with('success', 'Product deleted successfully.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            \Log::error('Product deletion failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->with('error', 'Failed to delete product. Please try again.');
        }
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/ProductController.php.bak =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\Site;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class ProductController extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $products = Product::with('materials')->orderBy('created_at', 'desc')->get();
        return view('products.index', compact('products'));
    }

    public function create()
    {
        $inventoryItems = Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get();
        $sites = Site::all();
        return view('products.create', compact('inventoryItems', 'sites'));
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        try {
            $validated = $request->validate([
                'product_name'  => 'required|string|max:255|unique:products',
                'description'   => 'nullable|string|max:1000',
                'category'      => 'nullable|string|max:255',
                'price'         => 'required|numeric|min:0',
                'stock_quantity' => 'nullable|numeric|min:0',
                'site_id'       => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
                'image'         => 'nullable|image|max:2048',
                'recipe'        => 'nullable|array',
                'recipe.*.inventory_id' => 'numeric|exists:inventory,id',
                'recipe.*.quantity_needed' => 'numeric|min:0.01',
            ]);

            DB::beginTransaction();

            $imagePath = null;
            if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
                $imagePath = $request->file('image')->store('products', 'public');
            }

            $product = Product::create([
                'product_name'   => $validated['product_name'],
                'description'    => $validated['description'] ?? null,
                'category'       => $validated['category'] ?? null,
                'price'          => $validated['price'],
                'stock_quantity' => $validated['stock_quantity'] ?? 0,
                'site_id'        => $validated['site_id'] ?? null,
                'status'         => 'active',
                'image_path'     => $imagePath,
            ]);

            if (! empty($validated['recipe'])) {
                $materials = [];
                foreach ($validated['recipe'] as $recipe_item) {
                    if (empty($recipe_item['inventory_id'])) continue;

                    $inventory_id    = $recipe_item['inventory_id'];
                    $quantity_needed = $recipe_item['quantity_needed'];
                    $inventory = Inventory::findOrFail($inventory_id);

                    if ($inventory->quantity_on_hand < $quantity_needed) {
                        throw new \Exception(
                            "Insufficient {$inventory->item_name}. Available: {$inventory->quantity_on_hand}, Needed: {$quantity_needed}"
                        );
                    }

                    $inventory->update([
                        'quantity_on_hand' => $inventory->quantity_on_hand - $quantity_needed
                    ]);

                    InventoryLog::create([
                        'inventory_id' => $inventory_id,
                        'type'         => 'used',
                        'quantity'     => $quantity_needed,
                        'reference'    => 'PRODUCT-CREATE',
                        'notes'        => "Material used for product: {$product->product_name}",
                        'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
                    ]);

                    $materials[$inventory_id] = ['quantity_used' => $quantity_needed];
                }

                if (! empty($materials)) {
                    $product->materials()->attach($materials);
                }
            }

            DB::commit();

            return redirect()->route('products.index')
                ->with('success', "Product '{$product->product_name}' created successfully! Materials have been deducted from inventory.");
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            DB::rollBack();
            \Log::error('Product creation failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()
                ->with('error', 'Failed to create product: ' . $e->getMessage());
        }
    }

    public function show(Product $product)
    {
        $product->load('materials');
        return view('products.show', compact('product'));
    }

    public function edit(Product $product)
    {
        $inventoryItems = Inventory::orderBy('item_name')->get();
        $sites = Site::all();
        $product->load('materials');
        return view('products.edit', compact('product', 'inventoryItems', 'sites'));
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Product $product)
    {
        try {
            $validated = $request->validate([
                'product_name'  => 'required|string|max:255|unique:products,product_name,' . $product->id,
                'description'   => 'nullable|string|max:1000',
                'category'      => 'nullable|string|max:255',
                'price'         => 'required|numeric|min:0',
                'site_id'       => 'nullable|exists:sites,id',
                'status'        => 'nullable|in:active,inactive',
                'image'         => 'nullable|image|max:2048',
            ]);

            if ($request->hasFile('image')) {
                if ($product->image_path && Storage::disk('public')->exists($product->image_path)) {
                    Storage::disk('public')->delete($product->image_path);
                }
                $validated['image_path'] = $request->file('image')->store('products', 'public');
            }

            unset($validated['image']);
            $product->update($validated);

            return redirect()->route('products.index')
                ->with('success', 'Product updated successfully.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            \Log::error('Product update failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->withInput()
                ->with('error', 'Failed to update product. Please try again.');
        }
    }

    public function destroy(Product $product)
    {
        try {
            if ($product->image_path && Storage::disk('public')->exists($product->image_path)) {
                Storage::disk('public')->delete($product->image_path);
            }
            $product->delete();
            return redirect()->route('products.index')
                ->with('success', 'Product deleted successfully.');
        } catch (\Exception $e) {
            \Log::error('Product deletion failed: ' . $e->getMessage());
            return back()->with('error', 'Failed to delete product. Please try again.');
        }
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/ProductionCapacityController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Product;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

/**
 * Production Capacity. Reads each product's recipe (product_raw_materials:
 * quantity_needed per unit) against current raw-material stock, and shows how
 * many units of each product can still be produced right now, plus the
 * ingredient that runs out first (the limiting ingredient). This lets the user
 * see producible output straight from inventory, instead of recording a batch
 * first and only then learning what stock allows.
 */
class ProductionCapacityController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $search = trim((string) $request->get('search', ''));

        $products = Product::with('materials')
            ->when($search !== '', fn ($q) => $q->where('product_name', 'like', '%' . $search . '%'))
            ->orderBy('product_name')
            ->get();

        $rows = [];
        foreach ($products as $p) {
            if (strtolower((string) $p->status) === 'archived') {
                continue;
            }

            $materials = $p->materials;
            if ($materials->isEmpty()) {
                $rows[] = ['product' => $p, 'producible' => null, 'limiting' => null, 'recipe' => []];
                continue;
            }

            $minUnits = null;
            $limiting = null;
            $recipe   = [];

            foreach ($materials as $m) {
                $needed    = (float) $m->pivot->quantity_needed;
                $available = (float) $m->quantity_on_hand - (float) $m->quantity_damaged;
                if ($available < 0) {
                    $available = 0.0;
                }
                $canMake = $needed > 0 ? (int) floor($available / $needed) : null;

                $recipe[] = [
                    'name'      => $m->item_name,
                    'unit'      => $m->unit,
                    'needed'    => $needed,
                    'available' => $available,
                    'canMake'   => $canMake,
                ];

                if ($canMake !== null && ($minUnits === null || $canMake < $minUnits)) {
                    $minUnits = $canMake;
                    $limiting = $m->item_name;
                }
            }

            $rows[] = [
                'product'    => $p,
                'producible' => $minUnits,
                'limiting'   => $limiting,
                'recipe'     => $recipe,
            ];
        }

        return view('inventory.capacity', compact('rows', 'search'));
    }
}

===== app/Http/Controllers/ProductionController.php =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\ProductionAudit;
use App\Models\RecipeVersion;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Gate;

class ProductionController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Yield preview: for every product with an active recipe, how many units
     * the CURRENT raw-material stock can produce (and which ingredient limits it).
     */
    private function yieldRows(): array
    {
        $versions = RecipeVersion::where('is_active', true)
            ->where('is_archived', false)
            ->get()
            ->keyBy('product_id');

        if ($versions->isEmpty()) {
            return [];
        }

        $products = Product::whereIn('id', $versions->keys()->all())->orderBy('product_name')->get();

        $invIds = [];
        foreach ($versions as $v) {
            foreach (($v->items ?? []) as $it) {
                if (!empty($it['inventory_id'])) {
                    $invIds[] = (int) $it['inventory_id'];
                }
            }
        }
        $inv = Inventory::whereIn('id', array_unique($invIds))->get()->keyBy('id');

        $rows = [];
        foreach ($products as $p) {
            $v = $versions[$p->id];
            $lines = [];
            $max = null;
            $limit = null;

            foreach (($v->items ?? []) as $it) {
                $i   = $inv[(int) ($it['inventory_id'] ?? 0)] ?? null;
                $per = (float) ($it['quantity_used'] ?? 0);
                if (!$i || $per <= 0) {
                    continue;
                }
                $on  = (float) $i->quantity_on_hand;
                $can = max(0, (int) floor(round($on / $per, 6)));

                $lines[] = [
                    'name' => $i->item_name,
                    'unit' => $i->unit,
                    'on'   => $on,
                    'per'  => $per,
                    'can'  => $can,
                ];

                if ($max === null || $can < $max) {
                    $max   = $can;
                    $limit = $i->item_name;
                }
            }

            if (empty($lines)) {
                continue;
            }

            $rows[$p->id] = [
                'id'     => $p->id,
                'name'   => $p->product_name,
                'recipe' => $v->name,
                'stock'  => (int) $p->stock_quantity,
                'max'    => (int) $max,
                'limit'  => $limit,
                'items'  => $lines,
            ];
        }

        return $rows;
    }

    /** Inventory > Production tab (yield preview + record form + audit log). */
    public function create(Request $request)
    {
        $yield   = $this->yieldRows();
        $isAdmin = Gate::allows('admin');

        $audits = null;
        $stats  = ['runs' => 0, 'flagged' => 0, 'missing' => 0];

        if ($isAdmin) {
            $since = now()->subDays(30);

            $audits = ProductionAudit::with('user')
                ->when($request->get('flag') === 'variance', fn ($q) => $q->where('variance_qty', '!=', 0))
                ->orderByDesc('id')
                ->simplePaginate(15)
                ->withQueryString();

            $stats = [
                'runs'    => ProductionAudit::where('created_at', '>=', $since)->count(),
                'flagged' => ProductionAudit::where('created_at', '>=', $since)->where('variance_qty', '!=', 0)->count(),
                'missing' => (int) ProductionAudit::where('created_at', '>=', $since)->where('variance_qty', '>', 0)->sum('variance_qty'),
            ];
        }

        $flag = $request->get('flag') === 'variance' ? 'variance' : 'all';

        return view('inventory.production', compact('yield', 'isAdmin', 'audits', 'stats', 'flag'));
    }

    /** Record a production run. Expected yield is computed by the server (recipe + current stock). */
    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        abort_unless(Gate::allows('admin'), 403);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'product_id' => 'required|exists:products,id',
            'actual_qty' => 'required|integer|min:0',
            'notes'      => 'nullable|string|max:500',
        ]);

        $actual = (int) $data['actual_qty'];
        $notes  = trim((string) ($data['notes'] ?? ''));

        try {
            $audit = DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $actual, $notes) {
                $product = Product::lockForUpdate()->findOrFail($data['product_id']);

                $version = RecipeVersion::where('product_id', $product->id)
                    ->where('is_active', true)
                    ->where('is_archived', false)
                    ->first();

                if (!$version || empty($version->items)) {
                    throw new \RuntimeException('This product has no active recipe.');
                }

                // Server computes the expected yield from CURRENT stock + recipe
                $lines    = [];
                $expected = null;
                foreach ($version->items as $it) {
                    $inv = Inventory::lockForUpdate()->find((int) ($it['inventory_id'] ?? 0));
                    $per = (float) ($it['quantity_used'] ?? 0);
                    if (!$inv || $per <= 0) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    $can      = max(0, (int) floor(round(((float) $inv->quantity_on_hand) / $per, 6)));
                    $expected = $expected === null ? $can : min($expected, $can);
                    $lines[]  = [$inv, $per];
                }

                if (empty($lines) || $expected < 1) {
                    throw new \RuntimeException('Current stock cannot produce even 1 unit of this product.');
                }
                if ($actual !== $expected && $notes === '') {
                    throw new \RuntimeException("Inventory says {$expected} should be produced, but you entered {$actual}. Please write the reason for the difference.");
                }

                // Materials issued for the full expected batch
                $snapshot = [];
                foreach ($lines as [$inv, $per]) {
                    $need   = round($per * $expected, 2);
                    $before = (float) $inv->quantity_on_hand;
                    $after  = round($before - $need, 2);

                    $inv->update(['quantity_on_hand' => $after]);

                    InventoryLog::create([
                        'inventory_id' => $inv->id,
                        'type'         => 'used',
                        'quantity'     => $need,
                        'reference'    => 'PRODUCTION-AUDIT',
                        'notes'        => "Issued for {$expected} x {$product->product_name} (actual produced: {$actual})",
                        'user_id'      => auth()->id(),
                    ]);

                    $snapshot[] = [
                        'inventory_id' => $inv->id,
                        'name'         => $inv->item_name,
                        'unit'         => $inv->unit,
                        'before'       => $before,
                        'used'         => $need,
                        'after'        => $after,
                    ];
                }

                $product->update(['stock_quantity' => (int) $product->stock_quantity + $actual]);

                $audit = ProductionAudit::create([
                    'product_id'        => $product->id,
                    'product_name'      => $product->product_name,
                    'recipe_version_id' => $version->id,
                    'recipe_name'       => $version->name,
                    'expected_qty'      => $expected,
                    'actual_qty'        => $actual,
                    'variance_qty'      => $expected - $actual,
                    'materials'         => $snapshot,
                    'notes'             => $notes !== '' ? $notes : null,
                    'user_id'           => auth()->id(),
                ]);

                \App\Models\FinancialAuditLog::record('production.recorded', $audit, [
                    'product'  => $product->product_name,
                    'expected' => $expected,
                    'actual'   => $actual,
                    'variance' => $expected - $actual,
                ]);

                return $audit;
            });
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', $e->getMessage());
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            report($e);
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Could not record production. Nothing was changed.');
        }

        $name = $audit->product_name;
        $exp  = $audit->expected_qty;
        $act  = $audit->actual_qty;

        if ($audit->variance_qty > 0) {
            return redirect()->route('production.create')->with('error',
                "VARIANCE: inventory should have produced {$exp} {$name}, but only {$act} were made ({$audit->variance_qty} short).");
        }
        if ($audit->variance_qty < 0) {
            return redirect()->route('production.create')->with('error',
                "VARIANCE: {$act} {$name} produced, " . abs($audit->variance_qty) . " MORE than the {$exp} expected.");
        }

        return redirect()->route('production.create')->with('success',
            "Recorded: {$act} {$name} produced, matches the expected {$exp}.");
    }
    /** Old "Production Audit" page now lives inside the Inventory > Production tab. */
    public function audit()
    {
        return redirect(route('production.create') . '#audit');
    }
}


===== app/Http/Controllers/ProductionController.php.bak-expected-auto =====
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Inventory;
use App\Models\InventoryLog;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\ProductionAudit;
use App\Models\RecipeVersion;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Gate;

class ProductionController extends Controller
{
    /**
     * Yield preview: for every product with an active recipe, how many units
     * the CURRENT raw-material stock can produce (and which ingredient limits it).
     */
    private function yieldRows(): array
    {
        $versions = RecipeVersion::where('is_active', true)
            ->where('is_archived', false)
            ->get()
            ->keyBy('product_id');

        if ($versions->isEmpty()) {
            return [];
        }

        $products = Product::whereIn('id', $versions->keys()->all())->orderBy('product_name')->get();

        $invIds = [];
        foreach ($versions as $v) {
            foreach (($v->items ?? []) as $it) {
                if (!empty($it['inventory_id'])) {
                    $invIds[] = (int) $it['inventory_id'];
                }
            }
        }
        $inv = Inventory::whereIn('id', array_unique($invIds))->get()->keyBy('id');

        $rows = [];
        foreach ($products as $p) {
            $v = $versions[$p->id];
            $lines = [];
            $max = null;
            $limit = null;

            foreach (($v->items ?? []) as $it) {
                $i   = $inv[(int) ($it['inventory_id'] ?? 0)] ?? null;

                if (!$i || $per <= 0) {
                    continue;

                $can = max(0, (int) floor(round($on / $per, 6)));

                $lines[] = [

                    'unit' => $i->unit,
                    'on'   => $on,
                    'per'  => $per,
                    'can'  => $can,
                ];

                if ($max === null || $can < $max) {
                    $max   = $can;
                    $limit = $i->item_name;
                }
            }

            if (empty($lines)) {
                continue;
            }

            $rows[$p->id] = [
                'id'     => $p->id,
                'name'   => $p->product_name,
                'recipe' => $v->name,
                'stock'  => (int) $p->stock_quantity,
                'max'    => (int) $max,
                'limit'  => $limit,
                'items'  => $lines,
            ];
        }

        return $rows;
    }

    /** Inventory > Production tab (yield preview + record form + audit log). */
    public function create(Request $request)
    {
        $yield   = $this->yieldRows();
        $isAdmin = Gate::allows('admin');

        $audits = null;
        $stats  = ['runs' => 0, 'flagged' => 0, 'missing' => 0];

        if ($isAdmin) {

            $audits = ProductionAudit::with('user')
                ->when($request->get('flag') === 'variance', fn ($q) => $q->where('variance_qty', '!=', 0))
                ->orderByDesc('id')
